# Skills · B · part 77

814 entries, book-intake to book_make. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **book-intake**: 家庭藏书入库技能。当用户发送书封照片、ISBN、或说「买了本书/入库/加书」时使用。调用 bookshelf CLI 完成识别、元数据补全与落库。 ([wangminle/home-bookshelf-management-v1](https://github.com/wangminle/home-bookshelf-management-v1/tree/HEAD/skills/book-intake) · ★1)
- **book-interp**: Read Winning 42 and a 42 net (Lem, Zeb's field net) as one discipline, the already-clean way. Use whenever a task reads a book rule, encodes one in Silas, measures a gap between the book and Zeb, matches book concepts against net units, or writes a book or Lem result; and whenever you feel the pull to write "the book is wrong" or "this unit is mixed". ([jasonyandell/mk5-main](https://github.com/jasonyandell/mk5-main/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/book-interp) · ★2)

- **book-kb-index**: Stage 5 of the book digitizer (supporting): load verified Knowledge Units into the memory layer — the lexical books.sqlite namespace (dz recall --books) AND the shared agentdb vector index (agentdb_pattern_search), so book know-how is recallable lexically and semantically across all digitized books. Upsert by (book, ku_id, corpus_version), never append-only. Triggers on: "index the book KB", "book-kb-index", "load KUs into memory". Invoked by `digitize-book`. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/skills-book-digitizer/book-kb-index) · ★1)

All processing is local — no external APIs. Use when user says "ingest this paper", "add this source to the book", "update the book wiki", "extract claims from this section", "audit the knowledge graph", "validate claims", "find contradictions in the wiki", "check what's stale", or asks to refresh sources for a chapter. Do NOT use for chapter drafting (use book-compose), prose rewrites (use russelli… ([CharlesHoskinson/russellian-book-suite](https://github.com/CharlesHoskinson/russellian-book-suite/tree/HEAD/skills/book-knowledge) · ★3)
- **book-knowledge-card**: 把一本书做成"小白能读懂的可视化知识卡片网页"（白板手绘风）。当用户想把一本书/一位作者的核心思想做成一张能直接看懂、能演示、能导出、能编辑的网页卡片时使用——它既不是纯文字读书框架，也不是通用结构化逻辑图，而是"书的内容可视化"。触发词：书籍知识卡片、把《XX》做成卡片/做成网页、用国富论那版方式做一本、书的可视化拆解、给小白讲清楚《XX》、书的阅读卡片、拆解一本书做成一张图。内容必含四块：①书籍定位（书名/作者/出版年/全称/一行核心问题）②当时的现实矛盾或旧观念（背景）③作者的核心内容与方法论（书中逻辑主线，结构可因书而异）④对读者的核心价值（能拿走什么、理解什么）。语言必须大白话、用具体例子、禁用抽象词；素材可来自用户笔记、微信读书热门划线，或只给书名自动调微信读书接口取重点。 ([hxcfindjob/book-distillery](https://github.com/hxcfindjob/book-distillery/tree/HEAD/skills/book-knowledge-card) · ★1)
- **book-knowledge-distiller**: 书籍知识规格化蒸馏与提炼顾问。输入书籍全文/章节/读书笔记，剔除 80% 的案例废话，精准提炼出 20% 的核心概念、心智模型与“行动启发式”决策规则。 触发词：「精读书籍」「提炼书籍知识」「distill book」「书籍概念蒸馏」「提炼这本书」。 ([xingkongzhixiac/agent-skills-hub](https://github.com/xingkongzhixiac/agent-skills-hub/tree/HEAD/domain-experts/book-knowledge-distiller))
- **book-knowledge-extract**: Stage 2 of the book digitizer: map-reduce the ingested corpus into page-anchored Knowledge Units (methodologies, decision-frameworks, formulas, heuristics, checklists, tradeoff-tables, case-patterns, definitions), then dedup/merge and run a KU-VERIFY entailment pass so only KUs actually supported by their cited pages proceed. Enforces the FACTS-verbatim / PROSE-paraphrase fidelity regime. Triggers on: "extract knowledge from the corpus", "book-knowledge-extract", "harvest KUs". Invoked by `digitize-book`. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/skills-book-digitizer/book-knowledge-extract) · ★1)
- **book-knowledge-graph**: 把书籍知识库（你的书籍库/）的书与核心概念构建成可可视化的「知识图谱 / 知识图谱」。核心数据是一个机器可读的全局关系网络 graph.json，图谱 Demo 直接消费它。触发词：书籍知识图谱、知识图谱、跨书关联、书与书怎么关联、把书做成图谱、图谱、graph.json、relation。同时作为 book-knowledge-card 产出后的强制联动环节——每做完一本书的卡片，必须增量更新 graph.json 并刷新 Demo（不要等所有书做完再补）。 ([hxcfindjob/book-distillery](https://github.com/hxcfindjob/book-distillery/tree/HEAD/skills/book-knowledge-graph) · ★1)
- **book-knowledge-map**: Process long books chapter by chapter and build a structured map of concepts, arguments, evidence, and cross-chapter relationships. - **book-knowledge-workflow**: 书籍知识库端到端总控 SOP —— 编排「输入 → 建卡(book-knowledge-card) → 提要点+分类(book-core-points + book-category-classify) → 建图谱(book-knowledge-graph)」全流程。触发词：书籍知识库流程、端到端建书库、拉书建卡建图谱、整库流程、批量梳理书、新增一本书全流程、书库怎么搭、图谱加卡片怎么组织、怎么开源部署书籍图谱。本 skill 不重复各子 skill 的写法纪律，只负责"按顺序把子 skill 串起来 + 对齐真实产物命名/铁律"，具体写法看被编排的子 skill。详细面向人的作见仓库根 `README.md`（与本文档同源）。 ([hxcfindjob/book-distillery](https://github.com/hxcfindjob/book-distillery/tree/HEAD/skills/book-knowledge-workflow) · ★1)

- **book2skill**: Use when distilling reusable analysis capability from a book — the "book2skill" pipeline. Orchestrates ingest → multi-perspective open-coding → atomic-method abstraction (with RAG firewall) → similarity clustering → playbook (orchestration) extraction → promotion, producing one attributed immutable run plus the book's playbook(s) and atomic methods. Triggers include "run book2skill on &lt;book&gt;", "distill skills from this book", "extract skills from &lt;book&gt;". - **book2skill-abstract-to-method**: Sub-skill of book2skill. Abstract a perspective's open-coding codes into atomic method cards, enforcing the RAG firewall (strip the book's examples, keep only the transferable method). These are atomic methods (the vocabulary), not assembled skills. Invoked by the book2skill orchestrator (Stage C). ([waynewangyuxuan/book2skill](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/book2skill/tree/HEAD/distiller/book2skill-abstract-to-method))
- **book2skill-ingest-book**: Sub-skill of book2skill. Convert a book file (epub/pdf) into anchored per-chapter text plus a one-paragraph thesis, as the source material for a book2skill run. - **book2skill-promote-to-registry**: Sub-skill of book2skill. Govern a run's raw outputs into the canonical registries — atomic methods to Methods/, assembled playbooks to Playbooks/ — collapsing duplicates, re-applying the firewall, and stamping provenance. Invoked by the book2skill orchestrator (final stage). ([waynewangyuxuan/book2skill](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/book2skill/tree/HEAD/distiller/book2skill-promote-to-registry))
- **book2skill-read-perspective**: Sub-skill of book2skill. Open-code a book through ONE reader perspective (drawn from the roster) into a codes.jsonl, applying novelty pressure and a saturation stop. Parametrized by perspective; invoked once per perspective (in parallel) by the book2skill orchestrator (Stage B). ([waynewangyuxuan/book2skill](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/book2skill/tree/HEAD/distiller/book2skill-read-perspective))
- **book2skill-synthesize-taxonomy**: Sub-skill of book2skill. Cluster a run's raw skills bottom-up into an emergent taxonomy, find cross-perspective merge groups, and write the run-log of process findings. Invoked by the book2skill orchestrator (Stage D–E). ([waynewangyuxuan/book2skill](https://github.com/waynewangyuxuan/book2skill/tree/HEAD/distiller/book2skill-synthesize-taxonomy))
- **book2skill-trace-playbook**: Sub-skill of book2skill. Reconstruct the book's analytical PROCEDURE (its orchestration logic) as a playbook — spine plus gated, dependency-ordered steps that reference the atomic methods. A control-flow / decision-tree reading, distinct from per-perspective open-coding. This is the high-value product. Invoked by the book2skill orchestrator.
